- **configure**: Compute a deterministic configuration plan for this repo, and optionally apply it. Each capability in the command registry declares a pure `planConfig` probe that derives its recommended settings from observable repo facts, so the same repo yields the same plan on every run and in every environment. - **connascence**: Grade the coupling between two modules (or two contexts) at the resolution of connascence. Works on whatever evidence exists — source code, a context map, a proposed contract, or a plain description — so you can grade a boundary even while still designing the contexts; every claim cites the concrete source it rests on, and that source-ref shows how solid each claim is. Runs as three numbered steps, each ending in a manual gate. Project-agnostic and stack-agnostic; drop it into any project. ([DomainDrivers/dd-ai](https://github.com/DomainDrivers/dd-ai/blob/HEAD/005/.claude/commands/connascence.md) · ★9)
